Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida
Since 2006
"Built on Legacy. Driven by Success."
Welcome to Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida
Since 2006
, the institute has been dedicated to providing quality education that inspires curiosity, strengthens practical skills, and prepares students to become confident professionals in a competitive world. Over the years, it has earned recognition as one of the
Best Engineering Colleges in Delhi NCR
, known for its strong academic environment and focus on career readiness. The institute is Affiliated with
Dr. A.P.J. Abdul Kalam Technical University (AKTU), Lucknow
and Approved by the
All India Council for Technical Education (AICTE)
, New Delhi and Accredited by the
National Assessment and Accreditation Council (NAAC)
and the
National Board of Accreditation (NBA)
, reflecting its commitment to high academic standards and institutional excellence. With modern infrastructure, advanced laboratories, and a learning approach that balances theory with hands-on experience, DGI continues to build a reputation as a
leading College for Placements in Greater Noida
and a trusted destination for quality technical education. The institution offers engineering and management programs designed to enhance critical thinking, innovation, and leadership abilities. Through industry exposure, practical training, and skill-development initiatives, students are guided toward successful careers, making DGI widely regarded as one of the
top academic institutions in Greater Noida
and a preferred choice for students seeking a
future-focused Engineering College in Delhi NCR
.
View More
Vision
Instilling core human values and facilitating competence to address global challenges by providing Quality Technical Education.
Our goal is to continuously improve the healthy learning environment and facilities being provided to the students to achieve academic excellence for employability as world class Technocrats and Managers, apart from making them noble human beings of a nation growing from strength to strength, dispelling the darkness of ignorance and ameliorating the society by means of philanthropic endeavours.
Goal
Mission
M1 - Enhancing technical expertise through innovative research and education, fostering creativity and excellence in problem-solving.
M2 - Cultivating a culture of ethical innovation and user-focused design, ensuring technological progress enhances the well-being of society...

AICTE Approval
Meet Us
The All India Council for Technical Education (AICTE) is the statutory body and a national-level council for technical education, under Department of Higher Education, Ministry of Human Resource Development established in November 1945 first as an advisory body and later on in 1987 given statutory status by an Act of Parliament.
Website Link
Approval Letter
AICTE is responsible for Promotion of Quality in Technical Education, planning and coordinated development of the technical education and management education system & Regulations and Maintenance of Norms and Standards in India.
Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida got first AICTE approval in 2006 & got extension of approval each subsequent year thereafter.

National Board of Accreditation (NBA)
The National Board of Accreditation (NBA) was set up in 1994 by the All India Council for Technical Education (AICTE) under Section 10(u) of the AICTE Act. Its main purpose is to evaluate the quality and effectiveness of technical and professional programs offered by institutions across India.
As the landscape of technical education evolved, there is a growing need for a more transparent and independent accreditation systems. Responding to this, NBA became a fully autonomous body on 7th January 2010. This transition allowed it to operate with greater neutrality and focus more strongly on outcome-based education ensuring that students not only gain knowledge but also develop practical skills relevant to industry needs. The Memorandum of Association and Rules of NBA were amended in April 2013, to make it completely independent of AICTE, administratively as well as financially.
NBA’s role goes beyond simple evaluation as it actively promotes quality assurance in technical education by accrediting programs in areas like Engineering & Technology, Management, Architecture, Pharmacy, and Hotel Management. The accreditation process is designed to measure how well institutions prepare students for professional challenges and global standards.
At
Dronacharya Group of Institutions
(DGI), NBA accreditation is granted to specific engineering programs which include:
Computer Science and Engineering (CSE)
Computer Science and Engineering and Information Technology (CSIT)
These accreditations indicate that the programs meet established quality standards in terms of curriculum, faculty, infrastructure, and student outcomes.

National Assessment and Accreditation Council (NAAC)
National Assessment and Accreditation Council (NAAC) was established by the UGC in September 1994 at Bangalore for evaluating the performance of the Universities and Colleges in the Country.
NAAC's mandate includes the task of performance evaluation, assessment and accreditation of universities and colleges in the country. The philosophy of NAAC is based on objective and continuous improvement rather than being punitive or judgmental, so that all institutions of higher learning are empowered to maximize their resources, opportunities and capabilities. Assessment is a performance evaluation of an institution and /or its units and is accomplished through a process based on self-study and peer review using defined criteria.
Accreditation refers to the certification given by NAAC which is valid for a period of five years. At present the Assessment and Accreditation by NAAC is done on a voluntary basis.
Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida, one of the Best Engineering Colleges in Delhi NCR is being accredited with A Grade by National Assessment and Accreditation Council in the session 2024-25 . The ranking is being awarded based on the quality of education and also for the contribution to the overall development of the students of DGI Greater Noida.

Learning Partners

Explore Partners
At Dronacharya Group of Institutions (DGI), Greater Noida, our Learning Partners form the backbone of a progressive and industry-aligned education system. Through strategic collaborations with leading organizations, professional bodies, and technology-driven institutions, we bridge the gap between academic learning and real-world application.
Our partnerships span across globally recognized organizations such as Cisco Networking Academy, Bosch, MSME training initiatives, Airports Authority of India, and professional societies like CSI and ACM, offering students exposure to industry-relevant tools, certifications, and practical training. These alliances enable hands-on learning, technical workshops, innovation-driven programs, and skill-based certifications that prepare students for evolving career landscapes.
By integrating academic excellence with industry engagement, we cultivate an environment where students don’t just learn but apply, innovate, and excel. From technical consulting programs to emerging technology platforms and experiential learning modules, our Learning Partner ecosystem empowers students to develop critical thinking, adaptability, and professional competence.
With a strong focus on continuous growth and collaboration, we remain committed to nurturing future-ready engineers who are equipped to lead, innovate, and make a meaningful impact in a rapidly changing world.

Important Notes
Working days shall be from Monday to Friday, along with the 1st and 3rd Saturdays of every month, for all students and staff.
Industrial visits are to be planned on Friday only.
Marks of Teacher’s Assessment in each Theory Subject would be adjudged based onCertifications/ Research Papers/ Coding Competitions. Separate file/notebook to be maintained for each subject.
Assessment of the Practical Subjects in the Labs will be on regular basis.
It is mandatory for all the students to appear for all the Internal Assessments (CIA /PUT) unless detained.
As per university guidelines, students must maintain at least 75% attendance in each theory and practical subject to be eligible for internal assessments (CIA/PUT) and subsequent end-semester examinations.

Anti Ragging Campaign
The very word sounds alien in the healthy and harmonious Dronacharyan tradition. We use the nomenclature ‘gurukula’ for our College and ragging is given a big ‘NO’ over here. The demon of ragging and the dread caused by it are kept far from the College Campus. We follow in word and spirit the Hon’ble Apex Court’s venerable decision in this regard.

Rules & Regulations
Discipline • Excellence • Integrity
Discipline & Conduct
Discipline is synonymous with the Dronacharyan tradition. Every care is taken to maintain an ideal discipline and to make it a primary part of our work culture. We believe that nothing substantive can be achieved without a true spirit of discipline. Our academic environment is nurtured on discipline. This happens to be the fore-most trait to enable us to impart quality education and to produce meritorious results. The entire code of conduct requires absolute sense of obedience and subordination to the Department Head and the Faculty concerned. However, any act of indiscipline, misconduct or irresponsible behaviour will invite strict disciplinary action, including expulsion from the Institute.
Dress Code
White Shirt & Black Trouser OR Sky Blue Shirt & Navy Blue Trouser with Formal Shoes.
The College follows a dress code for all students of B.Tech and MBA course on all official occasions such as Seminars / Workshops / Industrial Visits / Placement activities / Guest Lectures, etc.
On all such occasions, it is mandatory for students to abide by the dress code.
Attendance
75 % Attendance (*) is compulsory both in Lectures and Tutorials as per the University norms. Students falling short of the requisite attendance are detained from the Semester Exams.
*10% relaxation on medical issues & 5% relaxation on academic performance
Library Rules
Library card will be issued to all the students by the Librarian. Students will show his/her admission receipt in the library to get his/her library membership form. Filled up form will be deposited with the Librarian within three days. Two color photographs should also be attached.
Library Card is mandatory for issuing the books.
A student with a Membership Library Card is entitled to draw 5 books for 45 days from the Library and is personally responsible for the books so taken by him/her.
Personal belongings are not allowed and may be kept at the counter provided at the entrance of the Library.
Use of mobile is strictly prohibited inside the Library.
Notice Board
Students must take note of all notices displayed on the Notice Board for information and compliance. Failure to read the notices displayed will not be accepted as an excuse for its non-compliance.
Students must also visit the college web-site, https://gnindia.dronacharya.info/, from time to time and take note of all the information and circulars.
Financial Support to wining projects by the college
Project that wins First Position (National/International) will be awarded Rupees Twenty Five Thousand.
Project that wins Second Position (National/International) will be awarded Rupees Ten Thousand.
Project that wins Third Position (National/International) will be awarded Rupees Five Thousand.
Project Guide/Mentor will be awarded Rupees Three Thousand.
Sponsorship for International Travel for Research Paper Presentation
Travel and Accommodation Expenses of Students of 1st Year and 2nd Year along with Faculty Member for presenting paper internationally are sponsored by the college.
Eligibility Criteria
Travel Expense : AC III Tier Train Fare or any other mode of transport, whichever is lesser. (From Delhi to Nearest Railway Station of Venue and back).
Stay : Rs.1000.00 (Rupees One Thousand) per night (Day/s of event and Max. 1 day prior to the event).
Food : Rs.300.00 (Rupees Three Hundred) per day (Day/s of event and Max. 1 day prior to the event).
1st Prize Winners: 100% as per detail above.
2nd & 3rd Prize Winners: 50% as per detail above.
No reimbursement shall be given for consolation prize and mere participation.
Faculty members accompanying students on above mentioned trips shall be reimbursed total expenditure.
In order to encourage students to participate at different platforms for project competitions and paper presentations, following criteria has been framed for the reimbursement of expenses incurred by the students:
Prior approval from concerned authorities - (HOD, Advisor R&D and Director) is a MUST for participation.

Student Achievement
Mechanical Students Win ASHRAE Grant
Mechanical Engineering students of Dronacharya Group of Institutions are appreciated for securing the $4,937 Undergraduate Program Equipment Grant from ASHRAE for their project “Split Air Conditioning Test Rig.” This achievement reflects their dedication, innovation, and commitment to advancing practical learning in HVAC systems.
Student Achievement
ICTN 2026 held at IIT Delhi
Ms. Aadhya Singh (20481, AIML), Ms. Suhani Bhardwaj (20335, CSIT), and Mr. Hardik Dhingra (20414, IT) successfully presented a research paper at ICTN 2026 held at IIT Delhi, bringing recognition to the institution through their research and teamwork.
Student Achievement
Girls’ Power Triumphs at Code Slayer 2K25
DGI students Pooja, Jagriti, and Shruti won the Girls-Only Track at the prestigious 36-Hour Code Slayer 2K25 Hackathon by DevSphereIndia, held at NIT Delhi.
Student Achievement
Dev Bhoomi Cyber Police Hackathon 3.0
Nishchay Chaurasia (CSE 3rd Year) secured 3rd Prize at the Dev Bhoomi Cyber Police Hackathon 3.0, showcasing strong innovation, teamwork, and technical skills in a 36-hour challenge, earning a cash award of INR 50,000.
Institutional Achievement
NAAC ‘A’ Grade
Institution has received an ‘A’ Grade NAAC accreditation in Cycle 1, highlighting its excellence in academics and quality education.
Student Achievement
DGI Shines at Technoxian World Cup 9.0
Winners at Technoxian World Cup 9.0 showcased exceptional innovation and technical skills on the global stage.
Institute Achievement
Recognition for Virtual Labs Excellence
Being Awarded as the Best Engineering Institute for actively contributing to Virtual Labs Initiative.
Innovation
Design Patent Granted
Dr. Pawan Kumar Sharma has been granted a Design Patent titled “Mathematics Teaching Device for Student Education” by the Patent Office, Government of India
Faculty Achievement
Scholarly Publication Milestone
Dr. Mamta Bhardwaj has authored “Analytical Chemistry Theory and Practical,” a valuable resource for learners and researchers in chemical sciences.
Research Excellence
Global Icon Award for Research Excellence
Dr. Kaustubh Kumar Shukla has received the Dr. A.P.J. Abdul Kalam Global Icon Award for his outstanding research contributions.
Research Excellence
Commendable Research Award – DTU
Dr. Neelam Baghel was honored for impactful research contributions at DTU.
Student Achievement
NCAT 2025 Toppers
MBA students achieved top ranks in NCAT 2025, with AftabAlam securing 3rd place and Diwakar Kumar and Akshat earning 12th and 14th positions in Category 4.
International Exposure
Global Internship Opportunity
Ms.Teena Khorwal, from ECE has been selected for the Global Internship Program at Daegu Catholic University, South Korea.
Students Achievement
Top 30 Rank in Hackzilla Hackathon
Team Sate Spherefrom the Department of Electronics and Computer Engineering secured a Top 30 position in the 24-hour Hackzilla Hackathon organized by IIIT Sonepat Transit Campus, Haryana.
Student Achievement
Scholarship Award for International Research
Tiya Bisla and Ritik Shukla students of the Department of Computer Science and Engineering received a ₹50,000 scholarship for a Master Programme at Kyushu Institute of Technology, Japan.
Student Achievement
Best Design Award - India Design Week 2025
Nitin Rathour and Rishi Singhstudents of Mechanical Department won the Special Jury Best Design Award at India Design Week 2025.
Student Achievement
FOSSEE Internship Selection - IIT Bombay
Sakshi Pandey and Priyanka from the Department of Electronics and Communication Engineering (ECE), have been selected for the Autumn 2025 Semester-Long Internship under FOSSEE, IIT Bombay.

9910380105, 7982298022

Training and Placement Cell at Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida
plays a crucial role in preparing students for successful careers by bridging the gap between academics and industry. It provides continuous guidance from the first year through skill development programs, industrial visits, internships, and industry interactions.
The Cell focuses on enhancing technical, analytical, and soft skills through activities like aptitude training, resume building, and mock interviews, ensuring students are industry-ready. With strong support from faculty, alumni, and corporate partners, it consistently creates diverse placement opportunities and maintains an excellent placement record.

Vision and Mission
Applied Sciences and Humanities is dedicated to building a strong academic foundation in core areas such as Applied Sciences, Mathematics, and basic engineering principles. It equips students with the essential knowledge and analytical skills required to excel in their technical disciplines while also nurturing effective communication abilities vital for future engineering professionals.
With a welcoming and student-centric environment, the department ensures a smooth transition into college life, helping learners adapt confidently to new academic challenges. Guided by highly qualified and experienced faculty members, students gain the clarity, confidence, and competence needed to successfully navigate the evolving demands of their engineering journey.
Vision
To inculcate a strong foundation in budding technocrats in the field of basic sciences and technology empowering them to learn engineering better and contribute to make a better world.
Mission
M1: To provide a strong foundation of knowledge and practical skills enabling technocrats to utilize scientific principles to give solutions to complex engineering problems.
M2: To guide students towards self-directed learning, self-discipline, and active engagement through innovative teaching and learning approaches.
M3: To inculcate values and ethics in students and make them responsible citizens of India.

Faculty
Faculty of B.Tech APS
APS department boasts a diverse group of highly qualified educators who strengthen students’ foundational understanding in physics, chemistry, mathematics, language, and communication skills. With advanced degrees and rich academic backgrounds, the team led by Prof. Pawan Kumar Sharma bring both deep subject knowledge and practical insights into the classroom, helping students develop critical thinking, analytical ability, and professional communication from day one. Their commitment to research, participation in conferences, and engagement with students fosters an environment where learners are encouraged to explore concepts thoroughly and confidently tackle future engineering challenges.

Vision and Mission
Computer science is deeply embedded in our day to day life. Recent technologies starting from smart phones to online banking, from stock market transactions to air traffic control are all reliant on computer sciences. It is also key aspect that maintains the automated systems for predicting and analysing data such as any disaster related early warning systems which help in protecting communities.
Without computer science, communication networks would fail, financial systemswould freeze, and transportation would become unsafe. As Modern society depends on computing to function smoothly, which makes Computer Science one of the most essential fields in today’s world.
Vision
Empowering the future with innovative advancements in computer science technology, fostering human values and principles, through technical education.
Mission
Advancing computer science technology through cutting-edge research and education, driving innovation and excellence.
Promoting a culture of ethical responsibility and human-centered design in technology, ensuring advancements benefit society.
Empowering individuals with technical skills and values to create a sustainable and impactful future in the digital age.

Faculty
Faculty of B.Tech CSE (Computer Science and Engineering)
CSE department has a prominent number of well qualified faculty members with excellent expertise in academic, administrative and industrial backgrounds. This diverse team of educators is led by Prof. Bipin Pandey (HOD) supported by experienced team members. This team helps in creating a learning environment which bridges the technical knowledge with real world technical skills. The department focuses on student mentorship and teaching excellence for creating a positive environment and overall growth.

Vision and Mission
Computer Science and Information Technology (CSIT) serve as the driving force behind today’s digitally connected society. From operating cloud infrastructures and protecting sensitive information to enabling e-commerce, healthcare technologies, financial platforms, and smart urban systems, CSIT fuels innovation across every major sector.
Pursuing CSIT builds a strong command over programming, software engineering, networking, cybersecurity, and information systems. At Dronacharya Group of Institutions, the CSIT program goes beyond technical training, the institution nurtures analytical thinking, adaptability, and problem-solving skills essential for global careers. By blending foundational knowledge with practical exposure, the program prepares students to thrive and make a lasting impact in the constantly evolving digital landscape.
Vision
Promoting technologists by imparting profound knowledge in information technology, all while instilling ethics through specialized technical education.
Mission
Delivering comprehensive knowledge in information technology, preparing technologists to excel in a rapidly evolving digital landscape.
Building a culture of honesty and responsibility in tech, promoting smart and ethical leadership.
Empowering individuals with specialized technical skills and ethical values to drive positive change and innovation in the tech industry.

Faculty
Faculty of B.Tech CSIT (Computer Science and Information Technology)
CSIT department has a dedicated academic team that is committed to deliver high quality engineering education while handling most dynamic and influential fields of technology. With a combination of lectures, lab work, and mentorship, our educators led by Prof. Hirdesh Gupta (HOD) play a central role in preparing students for future careers in areas such as cybersecurity, intelligent systems, and digital innovation, ensuring that graduates are both industry-ready and capable of lifelong learning. Their teaching approach emphasizes not only core concepts like software development, networking, and information systems but also the ethical and professional skills needed to succeed in the rapidly evolving tech landscape.
Prof. Hridesh Gupta
(HOD)
Prof. Manoj Kumar Yadav
(Associate Professor)

Vision and Mission
Information Technology is a gateway to become a key driver of innovation in today’s technology-powered world. Every industry including healthcare, finance, education, manufacturing and entertainment, mostly relies on skilled IT professionals to build systems, streamline operations, and create smarter digital experiences. Information Technology engineers are the problem-solvers behind the scenes who turn ideas into impactful solutions.
This program offers a wide range of exciting career paths from Software Development and Artificial Intelligence to Data Science, Android App Development, and Database Administration, IT offers opportunities to explore, specialize, and grow in multiple directions.
Vision
To provide students with theoretical understanding and technical proficiency in Information Technology, instill with moral and ethical values, to excel in academic, industry, and research settings.
Mission
M1: To instill in students a strong foundation of both the theory and practical application of IT skills, combined with the innovation and research approaches to keep pace with emerging technologies.
M2: To empower graduates to become global leaders specializing in field of Information Technology.
M3: To impart to students the social, ethical, and moral values necessary for them to make substantial contributions to society.

Vision and Mission
Today’s digital scenario has grown beyond imagination these days. Electronics and Communication Engineering focuses on knowledge dispersal regarding semi-conductors, chip design, processor design, communication equipment's satellite, aerospace manufacturing etc.
At Dronacharya Group of Institutions, the engineers engage in research, designing, development and testing of the electronic equipment which are used in various communication systems.
Vision
Providing advanced education and engaging technocrats in cutting-edge research in field of Electronics and Communication Engineering and foster them to be globally competitive engineering professionals with a strong ethical foundation.
Mission
To deliver robust foundational knowledge and technical expertise through effective teaching learning Methodologies.
To create an environment conducive to research by fostering partnerships between industry and academia.
To integrate advancement of technology in Electronics and Communication Engineering with a focus on social responsibility and environmental consciousness.

Vision and Mission
Electronics and Computer Engineering is a cross disciplinary program that blends two dynamic domains and prepares you to thrive in an AI-driven future. Stay aligned with cutting-edge innovations at the intersection of electronic hardware and software while continuously sharpening your analytical and technical skills.
Enrolling in this course can lead to diverse career paths in areas such as VLSI design, the telecommunications sector, the Internet of Things (IoT), artificial intelligence, organizations like DRDO, and many other emerging technology fields.
Vision
To achieve excellence in Electronics and Computer engineering through quality education, research contributing to the emerging technologies and innovation to serve industry and society.
Mission
M1: To help students achieve their goals by recognizing, identifying, and to bring up their unique strengths through quality education and cutting-edge research training.
M2: To facilitate adequate exposure to the students through training in the state of-the art technologies.
M3: To imbibe ability in the students to solve real life problems as per need of the society through nurturing their skills, creative thinking, and research acumen.

Faculty
Faculty of B.Tech ECZ
ECZ department brings together a team of dedicated educators with strong academic credentials, including Ph.D. holders and experienced master’s degree professionals, alongside several pursuing advanced doctoral research.
The collective expertise spans core areas of electronics, computing, and interdisciplinary technologies, equipping students with both theoretical foundations and practical insights. The blend of seasoned scholars and research-driven faculty ensures that learners benefit from up-to-date knowledge, mentorship, and a mind-set geared toward innovation in rapidly advancing tech fields.

Vision and Mission
Electrical Engineering stands at the heart of our rapidly digitalizing world. From the smart devices we rely on every day to the large-scale power systems that keep cities running, electrical engineers shape the technology that powers modern life. Their work spans an incredible range starting from tiny semiconductor components to vast power generation and distribution networks.
The influence of Electrical and Electronics Engineering reaches into nearly every sector, driving innovation in manufacturing, energy, communication, business, entertainment, healthcare, transportation, and environmental sustainability. In essence, it is the force that connects ideas to real-world impact, transforming the way we live, work, and interact.
Vision
To be a Centre of Excellence in Globalizing Education and Research in the field of Electrical and Electronics Engineering.
Mission
M1: To empower technocrats with state-of-art knowledge to excel as eminent electrical engineers with multi-disciplinary skills.
M2: To emphasize social values and leadership qualities to meet the industrial needs, societal problems and global challenges.
M3: To enable the technocrats to accomplish impactful research and innovations.

Faculty
Faculty of B.Tech EEE
EEE Department is driven by a highly capable and experienced team of educators and researchers committed to academic excellence and student success. With a diverse mix of professors and assistant professors, the faculty lead by Prof. Sanghamitra V Arora (HOD) bring strong qualifications, industry insights, and practical teaching experience that enrich the learning environment. Their expertise spans core areas of electrical systems, power engineering, electronics, control systems, and modern technologies, ensuring students receive up-to-date knowledge and real-world perspectives. Through a blend of scholarly research, professional competence, and a passion for teaching, the EEE faculty play an essential role in shaping competent engineers ready to meet the challenges of evolving technological landscapes.

Vision and Mission
Mechanical Engineering powers innovation from Nano robots to aircraft engines at every scale. It is the dynamic field where electronics, physics, mechanics, and thermodynamics converge to design, analyse, and refine systems that drive the modern world. Every machine, structure, and process begins with the creative and analytical expertise of a mechanical engineer.
Mechanical engineers shape technologies that range from precision micro-sensors used in advanced medical devices to high-performance energy systems, automobiles, and aircraft. By combining engineering principles with advanced computer-aided design and simulation tools, they transform ideas into efficient, reliable, and ground-breaking solutions that keep industries moving forward.
Vision
To Become a Centre of Excellence in teaching and research in the field of Mechanical Engineering for producing skilled professionals having a zeal to serve society.
Mission
M1:
To create an environment where students can be equipped with strong fundamental concepts.
M2:
To provide an exposure to emerging technologies by providing hands on experience for generating competent professionals.
M3:
To promote Research and Development in the frontier areas of Mechanical Engineering and encourage
students for pursuing higher education.
M4:
To inculcate in students ethics, professional values, team work and leadership skills.

Faculty
Faculty of B.Tech ME
Mechanical department is a diverse and highly qualified team of educators committed to excellence in teaching, research, and real-world engineering practice.Our academicians bring rich experience from academia, research, and professional engineering roles, helping students build solid technical foundations. Our team led by Prof. Shailesh Kumar Singh (HOD) have strong competencies in areas such as design, production systems, materials science, automation, and thermodynamics, and actively participate in conferences, workshops, and publications that keep them abreast of evolving industry trends. Through hands-on guidance, mentorship, and exposure to both theoretical and practical aspects of Mechanical Engineering, the faculty play a vital role in shaping skilled engineers ready for careers in automotive, aerospace, energy systems, manufacturing and beyond.
Prof. Shailesh Kumar Singh
(HOD/Associate Professor)

Vision and Mission
CSE (Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning) is designed for students who aspire to build the intelligent technologies shaping the future. This program develops the ability to understand, analyse, and create smart systems in the form of advanced software, intelligent applications, or automated decision-making machines. Throughout the course, students explore how intelligent solutions are designed, trained, and deployed to solve real-world challenges across industries.
By combining theoretical depth with practical implementation, the program prepares graduates to step confidently into high-impact roles such as Innovation Engineer, Machine Learning Engineer, Applied AI Engineer, Data Science Engineer, Data Scientist, Data Modeller, Senior ML Developer, Head of Analytics (E-Commerce), and AI & ML Expert.
With the growing demand for intelligent technologies worldwide, this specialization opens doors to dynamic careers at the forefront of digital transformation.
Vision
Foster a collaborative environment and disseminate innovative solutions to academia and industry sectors through the application of latest tools in artificial intelligence and machine learning (AIML), aiming to contribute to the overall well-being of society.
Mission
M1:
Elevating the skills of technocrats with the use of modern and innovative infrastructure in teaching and learning process.
M2:
Offering high-quality ethics-driven education to acquire expertise in Artificial Intelligence and related fields, emphasizing research and innovation for the betterment of society.
M3:
Fostering the professional development of students by instilling ethical values and leadership skills, supported by collaboration with the industry.

Faculty
Faculty of B. Tech CSE (AIML)
CSE (AIML) department comprises of experienced and well qualified academicians who are committed to nurturing the next generation of AI innovators. This expert team of academicians lead by Prof. Ram Gopal Sharma (HOD) helps in creating a strong academic foundation in algorithms, data modelling, and machine learning techniques that enable systems to learn from data and make accurate predictions or informed decisions.
With diverse academic backgrounds and expertise in areas like AI, Machine Learning, and Advanced Computing, the faculty creates a dynamic learning environment that blends strong fundamentals with emerging tech trends. Our approach emphasizes not just teaching core theories but also inspiring creativity, research aptitude, and real-world problem-solving through interactive sessions, industry-relevant projects, and mentorship opportunities.
Prof. Ram Gopal Sharma
(HOD)
Prof. Vivek Krishna Misra
(Assistant Professor)
Prof. Ankita Sharma
(Assistant Professor)
Prof. Priyanka Asthana

Vision and Mission
MBA is a postgraduate qualification designed to shape confident leaders and strategic thinkers. It strengthens the understanding of core business functions, sharpens decision-making abilities, and expand professional networks. With an MBA, students position for higher levels of responsibility, accelerated career growth, and enhanced earning potential.
Whether the student background is in technology, finance, management, or manufacturing, MBA equips you with the insight and practical exposure needed to stay ahead in a competitive marketplace. It prepares the student to adapt, innovate, and lead making you truly future-ready in an ever-evolving professional landscape.
Vision
To be a leading centre of excellence in management education fostering excellence in education, research, consultancy, entrepreneurship, and innovation, with a strong commitment to sustainability.
Mission
Enhanced practical knowledge and exposure to the day-to-day operations of industrial settings.
Improved ability to bridge the gap between theoretical knowledge and real-world applications in the field of management.
Strengthened networking opportunities and potential avenues for internships or collaborations with industries.

Faculty
Faculty of MBA
MBA department brings together a dedicated group of educators with strong academic qualifications and diverse expertise in areas such as finance, human resources, management, and foundational business research. With several faculty members holding Ph.D. degrees and others possessing advanced master’s qualifications, they combine scholarly insight with real-world business perspectives to enrich classroom learning and student engagement. The team led by Prof. Shiwani Sharma (HOD), whose strengths lie in guiding students to understand complex management principles, develop strategic thinking, and strengthen communication and leadership skills which are all essential for future business leaders.
Through interactive teaching, mentorship, and professional experience, the MBA faculty support students in becoming confident, career-ready professionals prepared to face the challenges of a dynamic corporate world.
Prof. Shiwani Sharma
(HOD)
Prof. Sahab Singh
(Professor)
Prof. Anu Jain
(Assistant Professor)
Prof. Sarita Tiwari
(Assistant Professor)
Prof. Kawal Preet Sawhney
(Assistant Professor)

Centre Of Excellence <a href="#">content</a>t A Top B.Tech College Greater Noida
Academic Calendar
Centre of Excellence
Safety & Security

Alumni
Events
Centre of Excellence
Spotlights
iMac LAB
Previous
Next
iMac LAB
Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida has set up a state-of the-art “iMac Lab” consisting of latest gadgets. The lab aims to equip the young technocrats of various domains that drive the digital world like mobile computing, multimedia, cloud computing etc. The lab would provide great opportunity to the students to have hands-on-experience on the incredibly powerful Mac and iOS. This in turn encourages students’ to learn the latest technologies and start-up operations in the fields of social innovation, social change, and social entrepreneurship.
CISCO NETWORKING ACADEMY
CISCO NETWORKING ACADEMY
Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida in collaboration with CISCO has set up CISCO Networking Academy for its students. Cisco Networking Academy transforms the lives of learners, educators and communities through the power of education technology, and career opportunities learners learn to install, operate, configure and verify a basic IPv4 and IPv6 network including configuring a LAN switch, configuring an IP router, identifying basic security threats, understanding redundant typologies, troubleshooting common network issues, connecting to a WAN, configuring OSPF in both IPv4 and IPv6, understanding wide-area network technologies, and getting familiar with device management. It is an authorized centre to provide CCNA R&S certification course training.
Previous
Next
E-YANTRA LAB
Previous
Next
E-YANTRA LAB
E-Yantra Lab has also been established by the Electronics and Communication Engineering Department of DGI. It is sponsored by IIT, Bombay and gives an opportunity to gain knowledge beyond the AKTU Syllabus. The Lab consists of Fire Bird V Robotics Kit, Fire Bird V –
P89V51RD2 Adapter Card, SPARK V Robotics Kit, Kinect (X-box) sensor, KUKA model of Robotics and more. Students have made many innovative projects with the support provided.
CENTRE OF EXCELLENCE FOR SMART MANUFACTURING
Previous
Next
CoESM
Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida signed Memorandum of Understanding (MOU) with Ministry of Micro, Small and Medium Enterprises (MSME) – Technology Development Center (TDC) to establish Centre of Excellence for Smart Manufacturing (CoESM) - School of Metallurgy for Skill Development on 17th August 2016. The center is one its kind and first in NCR.
The CoESM is established with an objective to:
Make the youth of the country employable by imparting vocational training that bridges the competency gaps to enable the youth to get into the job from the day one.
Make the nation progressive by providing relevant training to the people who are at the bottom of the pyramid & thus eradicating the existing skill gap.
PCB LAB
PCB LAB
ECE and EEE Departments of Dronacharya Group of Institutions has established PCBLab. The PCB is widely used in fabrication of electronics as well as electrical circuits. The PCB Centre of Excellence at DGI, Greater Noida is equipped with latest equipment to meet the needs of research & development activities. We upgraded our laboratory with the latest equipment provided by PCB Lab which is a global leader in printed circuit boards designing and fabrication and also supplying machineries with raw materials to develop high quality industry standard printed circuit boards. This collaboration makes our students industry ready. Our lab supports up to eight layers PCB fabrication, each plotter comes with comprehensive LPKF Circuit CAM and Broad Master Software for importing PCB data from any CAD package. Students of DGI have a great opportunity to learn through the equipment of this Lab and make themselves Industry ready.
Previous
Next
SPOKEN TUTORIAL
Previous
Next
SPOKEN TUTORIAL
Spoken Tutorial is a multi-award winning educational content portal. DGI, Greater Noida has tied up hands with Spoken Tutorial where one can learn various FOSS (Free and Open Source Software) all by oneself. This is the First FOSS centre of UP in association with Spoken Tutorial Project, IIT Bombay. Spoken Tutorial Project is about teaching and learning a particular FOSS like Linux, Scilab, LaTeX, PHP & MySQL, Java, C/C++,LibreOffice etc. The courses offered are self-paced, multi-lingual and ensure that anybody with a computer and a desire for learning, can learn from any place, at any time and in a language of their choice. End-of-course online tests and certificates are available for those who wish to test their expertise in particular software. These certificates give an edge to students during placement by increasing their employability potential.
ADVANCED TRAINING CENTRE
ADVANCED TRAINING CENTRE
Dronacharya Group of Institutions has developed an in house Advance Training Centre for the benefit of its students. The training centre is equipped with all modern machineries and equipment for students learning. The centre is equipped with CNC Mill Trainer with Siemens Controller, CNC Lathe HPL 100 with Siemens Controller, BMW Twinpower Turbo In-Line 4-Cylinder 2.0 Litre Diesel Engine, PlC Training Equipment Set 15182546 (provided by FESTO). Students learn by doing self with the help of the trainers. The centre enables the understanding of the operation of CNC, Drive and PLC possible with diagnosis, data backup, programme development, operation simulation and more.
Previous
Next
HONEYWELL ICT ACADEMY
Previous
Next
HONEYWELL ICT ACADEMY
ICT Computer Training is renowned nationwide for the high quality of its education and training courses. Dronacharya Group of Institutions gives its students the facility to take the computer training course with ICT Academy at introductory and beginner level and gets progressed to advanced and expert levels. Students get benefitted by participating in online computer training programmes. The students have the opportunity to get certification that is recognized globally, to participate in various Hackathons and Empowerment Summit.
BOSCH TRAINING CENTRE
BOSCH TRAINING CENTRE
Bosch Joint Training Centre established jointly by Dronacharya Group of Institutions, Greater Noida
and Bosch Limited. The training centre is established as per BOSCH concept.
The center has a combination of
hardware, teach ware and courseware which is offered by BOSCH as industry oriented training in the field of automobile.
It imparts training to undergraduate, students to meet industry requirements,
bridging academic gap and enhancing the competency levels of students for better
career opportunities.
